\displaystyle[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}(\gamma\,\mathrm{d}_{A}\Omega-F_{A})]+\gamma F_{A}\wedge\tau=0\,,

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{A}(2\gamma\,e\wedge\tau-[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e])+\gamma[\Omega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]]=0\,,

\displaystyle\,\mathrm{d}_{A}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]=0\,,

\displaystyle\langle e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}F_{A}\rangle=0\,,

We also obtain the equations for

\displaystyle\begin{split}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}X_{A}]\!-\!\gamma\tau\!\wedge\!X_{A}\!-\!\gamma[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}X_{\Omega}]=&\,[(\gamma\,\mathrm{d}_{A}\Omega-F_{A})\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e_{\mathrm{t}}]+\gamma\,\mathrm{d}_{A}A_{\mathrm{t}}\wedge\tau+[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{A}A_{\mathrm{t}}]\\

(4.7a)

\displaystyle\begin{split}\gamma X_{e}\wedge\tau\!+\!\gamma e\wedge X_{\tau}\!-\![X_{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]=&-\,\mathrm{d}_{A}[e_{\mathrm{t}}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]+\gamma[(e\wedge\tau)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}A_{\mathrm{t}}]+[[A_{\mathrm{t}}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\r &\mbox{}\hskip-34.44434pt-\gamma[[\Omega_{\mathrm{t}}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\!+\!\gamma\,\mathrm{d}_{A}(e_{\mathrm{t}}\wedge\tau\!-\!\tau_{\mathrm{t}}\wedge e)\!+\!\gamma[\Omega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[e_{\mathrm{t}}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]]\,,\end{split}

(4.7b)

\displaystyle[X_{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]=

\displaystyle\,\,\mathrm{d}_{A}[e_{\mathrm{t}}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]+[[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}A_{\mathrm{t}}]\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\,,

\displaystyle\langle e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}X_{A}\rangle=

\displaystyle\,\langle F_{A}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e_{\mathrm{t}}\rangle+\langle e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{A}A_{\mathrm{t}}\rangle\,.

They can be significantly simplified by replacing the unknowns
(X_{e},X_{A},X_{\Omega},X_{\tau})
with
(Z_{e},Z_{A},Z_{\Omega},Z_{\tau})
according to

\displaystyle\begin{split}X_{\!A}&=\pounds_{\xi}A+\mathrm{d}_{A}\Lambda+Z_{A}\,,\\
X_{\!\Omega}&=\pounds_{\xi}\Omega-[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\Omega]+\mathrm{d}_{A}\psi+Z_{\Omega}\,,\\
X_{e}&=\pounds_{\xi}e-[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]+Z_{e}\,,\\
X_{\tau}&=\pounds_{\xi}\tau-\langle\psi\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle+\mathrm{d}N+Z_{\tau}\,,\end{split}

(4.8)

where we have used the shorthand notation

\displaystyle\Lambda

\displaystyle:=A_{\mathrm{t}}-\xi\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}A\,,\qquad

\displaystyle N:=\tau_{\mathrm{t}}-\xi\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\tau\,,

(4.9)

\displaystyle\psi

\displaystyle:=\Omega_{\mathrm{t}}-\xi\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\Omega\,,\qquad

\displaystyle\xi:=\langle e_{\mathrm{t}}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle\,.

Geometrically,
(\Lambda,\psi)
are the local gauge parameters that generate the internal gauge symmetries [see equations (
3.2
) and (
3.3
)], while
(N,\xi)
encapsulate the
4
-dimensional diffeomorphisms (they are related to the lapse-shift; we require
N\neq 0
).

The redefinitions (
4.8
) are dictated by the symmetries of the action and previous results on the Husain-Kuchař model
[
25
]
. The equations for the Hamiltonian vector fields (
4.7
) in terms of new variables
(Z_{e},Z_{A},Z_{\Omega},Z_{\tau})
are:

\displaystyle[Z_{A}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]-\gamma\tau\wedge Z_{A}-\gamma[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}Z_{\Omega}]=-\gamma NF_{A}\,,

\displaystyle Z_{e}\wedge\tau+e\wedge Z_{\tau}=-N\mathrm{d}_{A}e\,,

(4.10q)

\displaystyle[Z_{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]=0\,,

\displaystyle\langle e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}Z_{A}\rangle=0\,.

We sketch their resolution now.

Step 1:
Using Lemma
A.4
, we can solve equation (
4.10
) and obtain
Z_{e}=0
.

Step 2:
We solve now (
4.10q
) which, on account of the previous result, simply reads
e\wedge Z_{\tau}=-N\mathrm{d}_{A}e
. This is an inhomogeneous equation which can only be solved if the following condition holds (see Appendix C of
[
30
]
)

e^{(i}\wedge\mathrm{d}_{A}e^{j)}=0\,,

(4.11)

in which case the solution is (see
A.2
for notation)

Z_{\tau}=-\frac{1}{2}N\left(\frac{[\mathrm{d}_{A}e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]^{i}}{\mathrm{vol}_{e}}\right)e_{i}\overset{\eqref{sec_const_3}}{=}0\,.

This implies that the right hand side of (
4.10q
) must vanish too. Hence, recalling that
N\neq 0
everywhere on
\Sigma
, we have the additional secondary constraints
\mathrm{d}_{A}e=0
.

Step 3:
The new constraints imply that (
4.6
) and (
4.11
) hold. Moreover, we can simplify (
4.6
) as follows:

0=2e\wedge\mathrm{d}\tau+[\Omega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]]=2e\wedge(\mathrm{d}\tau+\langle\Omega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle)\,,

(4.12)

where we have used the identity
[\Omega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]]=-2\langle\Omega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle\wedge e
. It is straightforward to see now that (
4.12
) is equivalent to
\mathrm{d}\tau+\langle\Omega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle=0
.

Finally, noticing that
\mathrm{d}_{A}e=0
implies
[F_{A}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]=0
, we can also simplify (
4.6
) leading to the secondary constraint submanifold
\mathsf{M}_{1}
defined by

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{A}e=0\,,

(4.13a)

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\tau+\langle\Omega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle=0\,,

\displaystyle[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{A}\Omega]+F_{A}\wedge\tau=0\,,

\displaystyle\langle e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}F_{A}\rangle=0\,.

Notice that, as expected from the simplified form of the field equations, these (eventually final) constraints do not depend on the Immirzi parameter.

Step 4:
Before proceeding with the resolution of the equations for
Z_{A}
and
Z_{\Omega}
, we will write down the consistency conditions that the Hamiltonian vector fields must satisfy. These are
tangency
conditions that hold because the Hamiltonian vector fields must be tangent to
\mathsf{M}_{1}
. They are obtained by computing the Lie derivatives of the constraints along the Hamiltonian vector field. In terms of the variables
(Z_{A},Z_{\Omega})
and using the constraints, they are

\displaystyle[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{A}Z_{\Omega}]+[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[Z_{A}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\Omega]]+\mathrm{d}_{A}Z_{A}\wedge\tau+F_{A}\wedge\mathrm{d}N=0\,,

\displaystyle\langle Z_{\Omega}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle=0\,,

\displaystyle[Z_{A}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]=0\,,

\displaystyle\langle e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{A}Z_{A}\rangle=0\,.

These should be considered in conjunction with (
4.10
). The condition (
4.14
) implies
Z_{A}=0
. As a consequence, (
4.14
) is clearly true and (
4.10
) simplifies to

[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}Z_{\Omega}]=NF_{A}\,,

which is of the type that we have already encountered. Its solution is

Z_{\Omega}^{i}=-N\left(\frac{e^{i}\wedge F_{A}^{j}}{\mathrm{vol}_{e}}\right)e_{j}\rightsquigarrow Z_{\Omega}\overset{\eqref{eq: star B and B^T}}{=}-N(\star_{e}F_{A})^{\top}=-N(\star_{e}F_{A})\,.

Notice that this implies
\langle Z_{\Omega}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle=0
because, from
[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}F_{A}]=0
we get

\langle Z_{\Omega}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle=-N\left(\frac{e^{[i}\wedge F_{A}^{j]}}{\mathrm{vol}_{e}}\right)e_{j}\wedge e_{i}=0\,.

The previous results imply that the Hamiltonian vector fields must have the form given by (
4.8
) with
Z_{e}=0
,
Z_{A}=0
,
Z_{\tau}=0
and
Z_{\Omega}=-N(\star_{e}F_{A})
.

Step 5:
At this point, the only thing left is to check that the tangency condition (
4.14
) holds, which can be immediately seen by plugging the values of
Z_{A}
and
Z_{\Omega}
.

Summary of the Hamiltonian formulation:

The dynamics is given by the Hamiltonian vector field
\mathbb{X}
with components

\displaystyle X_{e}

\displaystyle=\pounds_{\xi}e-[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\,,

\displaystyle X_{e_{\mathrm{t}}}\text{ arbitrary}\,,

(4.15)

\displaystyle X_{A}

\displaystyle=\pounds_{\xi}A+\mathrm{d}_{A}\Lambda\,,

\displaystyle X_{A_{\mathrm{t}}}\text{ arbitrary}\,,

\displaystyle X_{\Omega}

\displaystyle=\pounds_{\xi}\Omega-[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\Omega]+\mathrm{d}_{A}\psi-N(\star_{e}F_{A})\,,

\displaystyle X_{\Omega_{\mathrm{t}}}\text{ arbitrary}\,,

\displaystyle X_{\tau}

\displaystyle=\pounds_{\xi}\tau+\mathrm{d}N-\langle\psi\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle\,,

\displaystyle X_{\tau_{\mathrm{t}}}\text{ arbitrary}\,.

It takes place on the submanifold
\mathsf{M}_{1}
of the configuration space defined by

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{A}e=0\,,

(4.16)

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\tau+\langle\Omega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle=0\,,

\displaystyle[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{A}\Omega]+F_{A}\wedge\tau=0\,,

\displaystyle\langle e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}F_{A}\rangle=0\,.

Recall that the gauge parameters
(\Lambda,\Psi,N,\xi)
are given by (
4.9
) and that the configurations
(e_{\mathrm{t}},e,A_{\mathrm{t}},A,\Omega_{\mathrm{t}},\Omega,\tau_{\mathrm{t}},\tau)\in Q
are subject to
e
being non-degenerate and
N\neq 0
. These open conditions guarantee that
(e,e_{\mathrm{t}},\tau,\tau_{\mathrm{t}})
define a non-degenerate cotetrad.

On
\mathsf{M}_{1}
, the induced presymplectic form (
4.4
) reads

\omega_{1}=\int_{\Sigma}\left(\langle\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\big(A-\gamma\Omega\big)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\big(\frac{1}{2\gamma}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\big)\rangle+\langle\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}A\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}(\tau\wedge e)\rangle\right)\,.

(4.17)

As in the covariant picture provided by equations (
3.9a
-
3.9
), the dynamics defined by the components of the Hamiltonian vector field can be interpreted as a combination of diffeomorphisms generated by
\xi
, internal
SU(2)
rotations with parameter
\Lambda
and Carrollian boosts with parameter
\psi
. In addition to this, there is non-trivial evolution defined by the two terms
-N(*_{e}F_{A})
and
\mathrm{d}N
present in
X_{\Omega}
and
X_{\tau}
, respectively. Notice that, at this point, the dynamics contains some arbitrary elements
X_{e_{\mathrm{t}}}
,
X_{A_{\mathrm{t}}}
,
X_{\Omega_{\mathrm{t}}}
and
X_{\tau_{\mathrm{t}}}
. We discuss this issue in the next section.

5
Time gauge

An interesting and useful way to disentangle the meaning of the Hamiltonian dynamics of the system is to introduce appropriate gauge fixing conditions. This procedure can be understood in the context of reductions in principal bundles. Here we discuss a version of the time gauge customarily used to study the Holst action of GR. The idea is to impose the condition

\tau=0

(5.1)

This immediately tells us that
X_{\tau}=0
which, in view of (
4.15
), leads to

0=\mathrm{d}N-\langle\psi\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle=\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{t}}-\langle(\Omega_{\mathrm{t}}-\xi\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\Omega)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle\,.

(5.2)

From this expression, we can solve for
\Omega_{\mathrm{t}}
and write it in terms of
\{e,\Omega,\tau_{\mathrm{t}},e_{\mathrm{t}}\}
:

\Omega_{\mathrm{t}}=\xi\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\Omega+(\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{t}})/\mathsf{det}\,e\,.

(5.3)

where we have introduced

\displaystyle\widetilde{E}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{\cdot\wedge[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]}{\mathsf{vol}_{0}}\right)\,.

with
\mathsf{vol}_{0}
a fiducial, non-dynamical volume form on
\Sigma
and
\mathsf{det}\,e
defined in Appendix
A.2
. The component
X_{\Omega_{\mathrm{t}}}
of the Hamiltonian vector field is no longer arbitrary and can be immediately derived from (
5.3
).

If we plug now the gauge fixing condition
\tau=0
into the presymplectic form
\omega_{1}
given by (
4.17
), we find

\overline{\omega}_{1}=\int_{\Sigma}\langle\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}(A-\gamma\Omega)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\right)\rangle\,,

(5.12)

whereas, plugging
\tau=0
into the constraints (
4.16
), leads to

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{A}e=0\,,

(5.13a)

\displaystyle\langle e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\Omega\rangle=0\,,

\displaystyle[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{A}\Omega]=0\,,

\displaystyle\langle e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}F_{A}\rangle=0\,.

The constraint (
5.13a
) has a simple interpretation:
A=\Gamma
where
\Gamma
is the Levi-Civita connection of the coframe
e
given by the condition
0=\mathrm{d}_{\Gamma}e=\mathrm{d}e+[\Gamma\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]
.

The symplectic form (
5.12
) can be simplified using
0=\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}(\mathrm{d}_{\Gamma}e)=\mathrm{d}_{\Gamma}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}e+[\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\Gamma\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]
and, hence,

\displaystyle\int_{\Sigma}\langle\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\Gamma\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\rangle

\displaystyle=2\int_{\Sigma}\langle\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\Gamma\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}[\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\rangle=2\int_{\Sigma}\langle[\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\Gamma\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}e\rangle=-2\int_{\Sigma}\langle\mathrm{d}_{\Gamma}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}e\rangle

\displaystyle=-\int_{\Sigma}\mathrm{d}\langle\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}e\rangle=0,

because
\Sigma
has no boundary. Using this once we plug
A=\Gamma
into (
5.12
) leads to

\overline{\omega}_{1}=\int_{\Sigma}\langle\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\left(\frac{1}{2}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\right)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\Omega\rangle\,.

(5.14)

or, in terms of
\widetilde{E}

\widehat{\omega}_{1}=\int_{\Sigma}\langle\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-1.00006pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-2.10007pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\wedge$}}\mkern-10.0mu\wedge$\cr\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d\!\raisebox{-0.05pt}{\scalebox{.8}[1.0]{I}}}\Omega\rangle\mathsf{vol}_{0}\,.

(5.15)

The constraints (
5.13
) in terms of the new variables are just simply obtained by writing
e
in terms of
\widetilde{E}
and setting
A=\Gamma
:

\displaystyle[\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-3.44444pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-3.44444pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-2.58334pt}{\kern-0.29999pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-2.15277pt}{\kern-0.29999pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\Omega]=0\,,

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-3.44444pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-3.44444pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-2.58334pt}{\kern-0.29999pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-2.15277pt}{\kern-0.29999pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{\Gamma}\Omega\rangle=0\,,

\displaystyle\langle\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-3.44444pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-3.44444pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-2.58334pt}{\kern-0.29999pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-2.15277pt}{\kern-0.29999pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}[\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-3.44444pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-3.44444pt}{\kern-0.50003pt$\scriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-2.58334pt}{\kern-0.29999pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}$\cr\raisebox{-2.15277pt}{\kern-0.29999pt$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}\cr}}}}F_{\Gamma}]\rangle=0\,.

Finally, we have to find the form of the components of the Hamiltonian vector fields that give the dynamics of
\Omega
and
\widetilde{E}
. In the case of
X_{\Omega}
it is immediate to get

X_{\Omega}=\pounds_{\xi}\Omega-[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\Omega]+\mathrm{d}_{\Gamma}\big((\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{t}})/\mathsf{det}\,e\big)-\tau_{\mathrm{t}}\ast_{e}F_{\Gamma}(\widetilde{E})\,.

(5.17)

In order to find
X_{\widetilde{E}}
, we use
2\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathsf{vol}_{0}=[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]
to get

\displaystyle X_{\widetilde{E}}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathsf{vol}_{0}

\displaystyle=[X_{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]=[(\pounds_{\xi}e-[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e])\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]=\pounds_{\xi}\left(\frac{1}{2}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\right)-\frac{1}{2}[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]]

\displaystyle=\pounds_{\xi}(\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathsf{vol}_{0})+[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}(\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathsf{vol}_{0})]=\Big(\pounds_{\xi}\widetilde{E}+(\mathsf{div}_{0}\xi)\widetilde{E}+[\Lambda,\widetilde{E}]\Big)\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathsf{vol}_{0}

where we have used the Jacobi identity (see Appendix
A.1
). The preceding result immediately implies

X_{\widetilde{E}}=\pounds_{\xi}\widetilde{E}+(\mathsf{div}_{0}\xi)\widetilde{E}-[\Lambda,\widetilde{E}]\,.

(5.18)

Notice that, at this point, there are still a number of dynamical variables with arbitrary dynamics such as
e_{\mathrm{t}}
,
A_{\mathrm{t}}
and
\tau_{\mathrm{t}}
that we must take care of. Before doing that, some comments are in order. First, the final form of the constraints is very similar to the constraints from which the Ashtekar formulation for GR is derived by the standard canonical transformation. In fact, the only difference is the scalar constraint (
5.16
). The usual procedure would then allow us to find an Ashtekar formulation for this model that will only differ in the form of the Hamiltonian constraint. In fact, the form of (
5.12
) directly suggests to introduce the Ashtekar connection
\Gamma-\gamma\Omega
which would be canonically conjugate to
\gamma^{-1}\widetilde{E}
.
Second, the Hamiltonian constraint found here coincides (if multiplied by an appropriate factor) with the extra term that must be added to the Euclidean Hamiltonian constraint in the Ashtekar formulation in order to describe Lorentzian GR. Finally, the constraints (
5.16
)-(
5.16
) coincide with the ones given in
[
17
]
after using the time gauge. Notice, however, that the approach that we have followed here differs in several significant ways from the one of
[
17
]
: (i) we use the most general Carrollian action that can be derived from the Holst action as shown in
[
13
]
, (ii) we use differential forms as the basic variables. This makes the Hamiltonian analysis much simpler and allows us to employ the powerful machinery of the exterior calculus. Notice also that in our approach we do not need to introduce the (awkward) quadratic constraint used in the literature to get the Hamiltonian formulation for the Holst action
[
18
]
.

As written above, the expressions for the Hamiltonian vector fields are actually quite complicated because they are expressed in terms of
\xi
,
\Lambda
,
\psi
and
\tau_{\mathrm{t}}
. Notice, for instance, that
\xi=\langle\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}e_{\mathrm{t}}\rangle/(\mathsf{det}\,e)
with
(\mathsf{det}\,e)
written in terms of
\widetilde{E}
.
This issue can be solved by imposing additional gauge fixing conditions. In particular, by choosing a smooth vector field
\hat{\xi}\in\mathfrak{X}(\Sigma)
on the spatial manifold
\Sigma
and requiring that
\hat{\xi}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}e=e_{\mathrm{t}}
, we see that the components (
5.17
) and (
5.18
) of the Hamiltonian vector field become

\displaystyle X_{\Omega}=\pounds_{\hat{\xi}}\Omega-[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\Omega]+\mathrm{d}_{\Gamma}\big((\widetilde{E}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathrm{d}\tau_{\mathrm{t}})/\mathsf{det}\,e\big)-\tau_{\mathrm{t}}\ast_{e}F_{\Gamma}(\widetilde{E})

\displaystyle X_{\widetilde{E}}=\pounds_{\hat{\xi}}\widetilde{E}+(\mathsf{div}_{0}\hat{\xi})\widetilde{E}-[\Lambda,\widetilde{E}]\,.

(5.27)

where there is still some arbitrariness as we have not fixed
\Lambda
nor
\tau_{\mathrm{t}}
yet. It is important to keep in mind that the condition
\hat{\xi}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}e=e_{\mathrm{t}}
gives
e_{\mathrm{t}}
as a function of
e
and tells us also about its dynamics because

X_{e_{\mathrm{t}}}=\hat{\xi}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}(\pounds_{\hat{\xi}}e-[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e])=\pounds_{\hat{\xi}}e_{\mathrm{t}}-[\Lambda\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e_{\mathrm{t}}]\,.

In order to reconstruct the original dynamical objects appearing in the action we need to know
A_{\mathrm{t}}
and
\tau_{\mathrm{t}}
. An important issue to have in mind is that, within the time independent formalism that we are using here, this is not simply a matter of choosing the remaining arbitrary fields as any time function we wish. Although it is true that there is some arbitrariness in them, the evolution of an object such as
A_{\mathrm{t}}
is given by an equation of the type

\dot{A}_{\mathrm{t}}=X_{A_{\mathrm{t}}}

where
X_{A_{\mathrm{t}}}
may be any sufficiently regular function
in phase space
. A possible and consistent choice would be a smooth, nowhere-vanishing function
\hat{\tau}_{\mathrm{t}}\in\Omega^{0}(\Sigma)
and
\hat{A}_{\mathrm{t}}\in\Omega^{0}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))
. In such case, the corresponding components of the Hamiltonian vector fields vanish (
X_{\tau_{\mathrm{t}}}=0
and
X_{A_{\mathrm{t}}}=0
). Another option for
\hat{A}_{\mathrm{t}}
would be to set
\Lambda=0
and, hence, the internal rotations disappear from the dynamics. Notice that it is only after this gauge fixing process that we get a simple interpretation of the dynamics defined by (
5
) and (
5.27
) in terms of the gauge parameters
(\hat{\xi},\hat{\Lambda},\hat{\tau}_{\mathrm{t}},\hat{\Omega}_{\mathrm{t}})
.

We end this section by emphasizing, again, that the presymplectic form (
5.12
) immediately suggests how to find a formulation for this model in the Ashtekar phase space for GR. As mentioned above, the Ashtekar variables can be directly read from
\bar{\omega}_{1}
. The computations leading to the Ashtekar formulation for this model are the standard ones, so we will not repeat them here (a simple account adapted to the language we use here can be found in
[
31
]
). Notice that this formulation can only be found because the action considered in the paper contains an extra term involving the Immirzi parameter.

6
Conclusions and comments

In this paper, we have studied the Hamiltonian formulation for the most general Carroll invariant action that can be obtained from the Holst action. For this purpose, we have relied on the GNH approach. When this method (or the essentially equivalent Dirac approach) is used, one of the key steps is to find the explicit form of the Hamiltonian vector fields that define the dynamics and check its consistency, in the sense that they must preserve the constraints. The resolution of the linear inhomogeneous equations for the components of these fields is usually hard, in fact, a brute force approach usually leads to unwieldy forms for their solutions that are hard to simplify by making use of the constraints. This complicates the interpretation of the dynamics.

A neat way to circumvent these difficulties can be found by looking at the geometric underpinnings of the model and the meaning of the Carrollian symmetry. A convenient setting for this is provided by Cartan geometry and the tools that it provides. By understanding how the symmetry works and the meaning of relevant geometric objects such as the torsion and the curvature, it is actually possible to guess some of the terms that are expected to appear in the Hamiltonian vector fields (
4.8
) and rewrite the relevant equations in terms of variables that simplify their solution in a significant way as shown in the paper. In particular, the expected gauge transformations originating in the Carrollian symmetry appear in a clear form.

We have also shown that it is possible to identify a dynamically determined congruence of curves such that (part of) the dynamics can be understood in simple terms because Lie dragging solutions to the field equations along these curves produces simple internal gauge transformations. This is similar to the behavior of the Husain-Kuchař model as discussed in
[
25
]
. In any case, for the magnetic Carrollian model that the action considered in the paper describes, the evolution
does not
simply reduce to internal rotations or Carrollian boosts, in particular for the
\Omega
field. It is noteworthy to point out that the torsion vanishes.

From the perspective of the classical dynamics, our description of the system is complete: once the initial data for the basic fields is prescribed in such a way that they satisfy all the constraints, their evolution is dictated by the Hamiltonian vector field given there. On the other hand, if one is interested in the Dirac quantization of the model, it is necessary to find a symplectic formulation such that the symplectic form takes the simplest possible form (in practice, it must have the canonical
\mathrm{d}q\wedge\mathrm{d}p
form). This requires some extra work. For the model discussed here, the time gauge that is successfully used for this purpose in other settings (for instance, when dealing with the Holst action) can be used to get a simple formulation as shown in Section
5.1
. Furthermore, by following the same steps leading to the Ashtekar formulation for GR, it is possible to find an Ashtekar formulation for the model. This may help quantize it and illuminate the harder problem of quantizing GR.
