Appendix A
Some useful mathematical results

A.1
Algebraic foundations and vector-valued forms

Let
\Omega^{p}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))
denote the space of
\mathfrak{su}(2)
-valued
p
-forms on
\Sigma
. Given a basis
\{J_{i}\}_{i=1}^{3}
of
\mathfrak{su}(2)
satisfying
[J_{i},J_{j}]=\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}J_{k}
, any
\alpha\in\Omega^{p}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))
expands as
\alpha=\alpha^{i}J_{i}
, where
\alpha^{i}\in\Omega^{p}(\Sigma)
.

We equip
\mathfrak{su}(2)
with an inner product
\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle:=-\frac{1}{2}K(\cdot,\cdot)
, where
K
is the standard Cartan-Killing metric. In our chosen basis,
\langle J_{i},J_{j}\rangle=\delta_{ij}
. The exterior product and the Lie algebra bracket extend naturally to
\mathfrak{su}(2)
-valued forms:

\displaystyle\langle\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta\rangle

\displaystyle:=\alpha^{i}\wedge\beta^{j}\langle J_{i},J_{j}\rangle=\alpha^{i}\wedge\beta_{i}\in\Omega^{a+b}(\Sigma)\,,

\displaystyle[\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta]

\displaystyle:=\alpha^{i}\wedge\beta^{j}[J_{i},J_{j}]=\alpha^{i}\wedge\beta^{j}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}J_{k}\in\Omega^{a+b}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))\,.

Let
A\in\Omega^{1}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))
be a connection 1-form. The associated gauge-covariant exterior derivative
\mathrm{d}_{A}
and its curvature
F_{A}\in\Omega^{2}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))
are defined by

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{A}\beta

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{d}\beta+[A\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta]\,,

\displaystyle F_{A}

\displaystyle:=\mathrm{d}A+\frac{1}{2}[A\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}A]\,.

Finally, for any
\alpha\in\Omega^{a}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))
,
\beta\in\Omega^{b}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))
,
\gamma\in\Omega^{c}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))
, and
\xi\in\mathfrak{X}(\Sigma)
, the following identities hold by virtue of the graded Jacobi identity and the invariance of the inner product:

1.

\langle\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta\rangle=(-1)^{ab}\langle\beta\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\alpha\rangle

2.

[\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta]=(-1)^{ab+1}[\beta\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\alpha]

3.

\langle[\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta]\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\gamma\rangle=\langle\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[\beta\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\gamma]\rangle

4.

\mathrm{d}_{A}[\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta]=[\mathrm{d}_{A}\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta]+(-1)^{a}[\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{A}\beta]

5.

\mathrm{d}_{A}\langle\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta\rangle=\langle\mathrm{d}_{A}\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta\rangle+(-1)^{a}\langle\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{A}\beta\rangle

6.

\xi\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}[\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta]=[(\xi\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\alpha)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta]+(-1)^{a}[\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}(\xi\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\beta)]

7.

\mathrm{d}_{A}^{2}\alpha=[F_{A}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\alpha]
and
\mathrm{d}_{A}F_{A}=0
(Ricci and Bianchi identities)

8.

[\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[\beta\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\gamma]]=(-1)^{bc}\langle\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\gamma\rangle\wedge\beta-\langle\alpha\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\beta\rangle\wedge\gamma

A.2
Top forms and frame dualities

Let
\Omega^{3}(\Sigma)
be the space of top-forms on
\Sigma
and consider a reference volume form
\mathsf{vol}_{0}\in\Omega^{3}(\Sigma)
. The quotient of any top-form
\mu\in\Omega^{3}(\Sigma)
with respect to
\mathsf{vol}_{0}
is uniquely defined as the smooth scalar function
\left(\frac{\mu}{\mathsf{vol}_{0}}\right)=f
satisfying
\mu=f\mathsf{vol}_{0}
.

Let
e^{i}\in\Omega^{1}(\Sigma)
,
i=1,2,3
, be a non-degenerate spatial cotriad. The dynamical spatial volume form
\mathsf{vol}_{e}
and its corresponding density factor
\mathsf{det}\,e
are given by

\mathsf{vol}_{e}:=\frac{1}{3!}\epsilon_{ijk}e^{i}\wedge e^{j}\wedge e^{k}\,,\quad\mathsf{det}\,e:=\left(\frac{\mathsf{vol}_{e}}{\mathsf{vol}_{0}}\right)\,.

(A.33)

Let
H:=\frac{1}{2}[e\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\in\Omega^{2}(\Sigma,\mathfrak{su}(2))
. We introduce the dual vector fields
\vec{E}^{i}\in\mathfrak{X}(\Sigma)
and their densitized counterpart
\widetilde{E}^{i}
relative to
\mathsf{vol}_{0}
via the relations:

\vec{E}^{i}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{vol}_{e}:=H^{i}\,,\quad\widetilde{E}^{i}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{vol}_{0}:=H^{i}\,.

(A.34)

These definitions yield the normalization
\vec{E}^{i}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,e_{j}=\delta^{i}_{j}
and imply
\widetilde{E}^{i}=(\mathsf{det}\,e)\vec{E}^{i}
. By using these equations repeteadly, one gets:

\displaystyle e^{i}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2\,\mathsf{det}\,e}\epsilon^{ijk}\widetilde{E}_{j}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\widetilde{E}_{k}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{vol}_{0}\,,

(A.35)

\displaystyle(\mathsf{det}\,e)^{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{3!}\epsilon^{ijk}\widetilde{E}_{i}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\widetilde{E}_{j}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\widetilde{E}_{k}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{vol}_{0}\,.

(A.36)

A.3
Simplification of the field equations

We establish here the equivalence between the field equations obtained directly by varying the action and their simplified forms.

Proposition A.1

The system of equations

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{e}]=0\,,

\displaystyle 2\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}(\uptau\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{e})-[\Upomega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{e}]]=0\,,

is equivalent to

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}\mathsf{e}=0\,,

(A.38a)

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\uptau+\langle\Upomega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{e}\rangle=0\,.

Proof.
It is straightforward to check that (
A.38
) implies (
A.37
). To prove the converse, we span
\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}\mathsf{e}
and
\mathrm{d}\uptau
in the basis components provided by the coframe
\{\uptau,\mathsf{e}^{i}\}
:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}\mathsf{e}^{i}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\alpha^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\alpha^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\alpha^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\alpha^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}\mathsf{e}^{j}\wedge\uptau+\mathchoice{\beta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\beta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\beta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\beta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{p}{q}}}}{\epsilon^{{{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.12234pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.46532pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.76094pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.91634pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.36322pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.688pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\math (A.39a)

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\uptau

\displaystyle=s_{i}\mathsf{e}^{i}\wedge\uptau+m^{i}\epsilon_{ijk}\mathsf{e}^{j}\wedge\mathsf{e}^{k}\,,

(A.39b)

\displaystyle\Upomega^{i}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\Upomega^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\Upomega^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\Upomega^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\Upomega^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}\mathsf{e}^{j}+\phi^{i}\uptau\,.

(A.39c)

Substituting (
A.39a
) into (
A.37
) we get
\epsilon_{ijk}(\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}\mathsf{e}^{j})\wedge\mathsf{e}^{k}=0
. Wedging this expression with
\uptau
yields
\epsilon_{ijk}\beta^{jk}=0
, while wedging with
\mathsf{e}^{r}
yields
\mathchoice{\alpha^{{{r}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\alpha^{{{r}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\alpha^{{{r}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\alpha^{{{r}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}-\mathchoice{\delta^{{{r}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.92825pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.34679pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.67627pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{\delta^{{{r}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$} , which implies
\mathchoice{\alpha^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\alpha^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\alpha^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\alpha^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}=0
.

Next, substituting these conditions into (
A.37
) and exploiting the algebraic identity
-[\Upomega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{e}]]=2\langle\Upomega\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{e}\rangle\wedge\mathsf{e}
, we get:

-s_{j}\uptau\wedge\mathsf{e}^{j}\wedge\mathsf{e}^{i}+m_{j}\epsilon^{jk\ell}\mathsf{e}_{k}\wedge\mathsf{e}_{\ell}\wedge\mathsf{e}^{i}-\mathchoice{\beta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\beta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\beta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}{\beta^{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}}\epsilon^{jk\ell}\uptau\wedge\mathsf{e}_{k}\wedge\mathsf{e}_{\ell}-\Upomega^{jk}\mathsf{e}_{j}\wedge\mathsf{e}_{k}\wedge\mathsf{e}^{i}+\phi^{j}\uptau\wedge\mathsf{e}_{j}\wedge\mathsf{e}^{i}=0\,.

(A.40)

Wedging from the left with
\uptau
gives
2m_{i}-\epsilon_{ijk}\Upomega^{jk}=0
, while wedging from the right with
\mathsf{e}^{p}
we get
\epsilon^{ipk}s_{k}+2\beta^{ip}-\epsilon^{ipk}\phi_{k}=0
. Contracting this final relation with
\epsilon_{ip\ell}
and using
\epsilon^{ijk}\beta_{jk}=0
yields
s_{i}=\phi_{i}
, which implies
\beta_{ij}=0
. Plugging
\alpha_{ij}=0
,
\beta_{ij}=0
,
s_{i}=\phi_{i}
and
2m_{i}=\varepsilon_{ijk}\Omega^{jk}
into (
A.39a
) and (
A.39b
) we immediately arrive at (
A.38a
) and (
A.38
).

Proposition A.2

If
\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}\mathsf{e}=0
holds, the field equation

[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}(\kappa F_{\mathsf{A}}-\gamma\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}\Upomega)]=\gamma F_{\mathsf{A}}\wedge\tau

(A.41)

is equivalent to

F_{\mathsf{A}}\wedge\uptau+[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}\Upomega]=0\,.

(A.42)

Proof.
Since
\mathrm{d}_{\mathsf{A}}\mathsf{e}=0
implies the
[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}F_{\mathsf{A}}]=0
, the term proportional to
\kappa
vanishes. As
\gamma\neq 0
we arrive at (
A.42
).

A.4
Some useful lemmas on frame contractions

Lemma A.3

Let
\mathsf{B}\in\Omega^{2}(M,\mathfrak{su}(2))
on a 4-dimensional manifold
M
. Let
(\mathsf{u},\mathsf{E}_{i})
be the frame field dual to the spacetime coframe
(\uptau,\mathsf{e}^{i})
, such that
\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\uptau=1
and
\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{e}^{i}=0
. If
\mathsf{B}
satisfies
[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{B}]=0
then

\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{B}=0\,,\qquad\mathsf{B}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\mathsf{e}^{i}\wedge\mathsf{e}^{j}J_{k}

(A.43)

and (see (
3.10
) for the notation)

(*_{\mathsf{e}}\mathsf{B})=(*_{\mathsf{e}}\mathsf{B})^{\top}\,.

(A.44)

Proof.
Let us introduce the 4-dimensional spacetime volume form
\mathsf{vol}:=\tau\wedge\mathsf{vol}_{e}
. Since
\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{e}^{i}=0
, contracting the condition
[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{B}]=0
with the vector field
\mathsf{u}
yields
[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}(\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{B})]=0
. Expanding the 1-form
\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{B}=(\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{B})^{k}J_{k}
on the local coframe basis gives:

(\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathsf{B})^{k}=b^{k}\tau+\mathchoice{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}e^{\ell}\,.

(A.45)

Contracting a second time yields
\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,(\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{B})^{k}=0
, which sets
b^{k}=0
. The vanishing bracket condition then simplifies to
\epsilon_{ijk}\mathchoice{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}\mathsf{e}^{j}\wedge\mathsf{e}^{\ell}=0
. Taking the wedge product of this equation with
\uptau\wedge\mathsf{e}^{m}
turns the constraint into:

\epsilon_{ijk}\mathchoice{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}\,\uptau\wedge\mathsf{e}^{m}\wedge\mathsf{e}^{j}\wedge\mathsf{e}^{\ell}=0\Rightarrow\epsilon_{ijk}\varepsilon^{mj\ell}\mathchoice{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}}=0\,.

(A.46)

Using the standard identity
\epsilon_{ijk}\epsilon^{mj\ell}=\delta_{i}^{\ell}\delta_{k}^{m}-\delta_{i}^{m}\delta_{k}^{\ell}
, we find
\delta_{i}^{m}\mathchoice{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}{b^{{{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}}-\mathchoice{b^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{b^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{b^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{b^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}=0
. Tracing this relation implies
\mathchoice{b^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{b^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{b^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}{b^{{{m}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09612pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.30226pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.07304pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}}=0
, which proves that
\mathsf{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\,\mathsf{B}=0
. This condition implies that

\mathsf{B}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}}}_{{{i}{j}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834 From

0=[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{B}]=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}_{{{j}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}_{{{j}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.34726pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.04167pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.45834pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\mathsf{B}^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\ell}}}_{{{j}{k}\mathchoice{\makebo it follows that

\star_{\mathsf{e}}\mathsf{B}=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{k}{l}}}_{{{i}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\texts ][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{j}}}_{{{k}{l}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.71356pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.29834pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.64166pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}(\mathsf{e}^{i}J_{j})=\frac{1}{2}\mathchoice{\epsilon^{{{i}{k}{l}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}{k}{l}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.56946pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.55847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.11319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\epsilon^{{{i}{k}{l}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\ By a similar argument, we can prove the following

Lemma A.4

The equation

[\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{X}]=\mathsf{C},

where
\mathsf{X}\in\Omega^{1}(M,\mathfrak{su}(2))
satisfies

\mathrm{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathsf{X}=0
, admits a solution if and only if

\mathrm{u}\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathsf{C}=0
. In this case, the solution is unique and is given by

\mathsf{X}=\frac{1}{2}\bigl(*_{\mathsf{e}}\langle\mathsf{e}\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\mathsf{C}\rangle\bigr)\mathsf{e}-(*_{\mathsf{e}}\mathsf{C})^{\top}.

Appendix B
Spacetime diffeomorphisms and gauge invariance

In this section, we explicitly compute the transformations of the spacetime fields
(\mathsf{A},\Upomega,\mathsf{e},\uptau)
under a spacetime vector field decomposed relative to a spatial hypersurface. Let
\jmath:\Sigma\hookrightarrow M
be the embedding map of the spatial hypersurface, and consider a generic vector field
\upxi
over
\jmath
which can be decomposed as

\upxi=\upalpha\partial_{t}+\jmath_{*}\beta\,,

(B.1)

where
\upalpha
is a lapse-like scalar function,
\partial_{t}
is the time-evolution vector field transverse to
\jmath(\Sigma)
, and
\beta\in\mathfrak{X}(\Sigma)
is the spatial shift vector. Under the structural decomposition of the fields, the time derivatives are governed by combinations of spatial Lie derivatives along a reference vector field
\xi\in\mathfrak{X}(\Sigma)
and internal gauge transformations.

By expanding the spacetime Lie derivatives and using the pullback map
\jmath^{*}
, we isolate the spatial configurations from their transverse/temporal counterparts.

Transformation of the connection
\mathsf{A}
:

\displaystyle\jmath^{*}(\pounds_{\upxi}\mathsf{A})

\displaystyle=\jmath^{*}((\upalpha\partial_{t})\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathrm{d}\mathsf{A}+\mathrm{d}((\upalpha\partial_{t})\mathbin{\smash{\raisebox{8.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-2.5]{$\neg\,$}}}}\mathsf{A})+\jmath^{*}(\pounds_{\jmath_{*}\beta}\mathsf{A})

\displaystyle=\alpha\dot{A}+A_{\mathrm{t}}\mathrm{d}\alpha+\pounds_{\beta}A\overset{\eqref{vector_fields_2}}{=}

\displaystyle=\alpha(\pounds_{\xi}A+\mathrm{d}_{A}\Lambda)+\pounds_{\beta}A+A_{\mathrm{t}}\mathrm{d}\alpha

\displaystyle=\pounds_{\alpha\xi+\beta}A+\mathrm{d}_{A}(\alpha\Lambda)\overset{\eqref{vector_fields_2}}{=}\dot{A}|_{\xi\mapsto\alpha\xi+\beta,\Lambda\mapsto\alpha\Lambda}\,.

Transformation of the cotriad
\mathbf{e}
:

\displaystyle\jmath^{*}(\pounds_{\upxi}\mathsf{e})

\displaystyle=\alpha\dot{e}+e_{\mathrm{t}}\mathrm{d}\alpha+\pounds_{\beta}e\overset{\eqref{vector_fields_2}}{=}

\displaystyle=\pounds_{\alpha\xi+\beta}e-[(\alpha\Lambda)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e]\overset{\eqref{vector_fields_2}}{=}\dot{e}|_{\xi\mapsto\alpha\xi+\beta,\Lambda\mapsto\alpha\Lambda}\,.

Transformation of the connection component
\Upomega
:

\displaystyle\jmath^{*}(\pounds_{\upxi}\Upomega)

\displaystyle=\alpha\dot{\Omega}+\Omega_{\mathrm{t}}\mathrm{d}\alpha+\pounds_{\beta}\Omega\overset{\eqref{vector_fields_2}}{=}

\displaystyle=\pounds_{\alpha\xi+\beta}\Omega-[(\alpha\Lambda)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}\Omega]-\alpha N(*_{e}F_{A})+\mathrm{d}_{A}(\alpha\psi)\overset{\eqref{vector_fields_2}}{=}\dot{\Omega}|_{\xi\mapsto\alpha\xi+\beta,\Lambda\mapsto\alpha\Lambda,\psi\mapsto\alpha\psi,N\mapsto\alpha N}\,.

Transformation of the clock field
\uptau
:

\displaystyle\jmath^{*}(\pounds_{\upxi}\uptau)

\displaystyle=\alpha\dot{\tau}+\pounds_{\beta}\tau+\tau_{\mathrm{t}}\mathrm{d}\alpha\overset{\eqref{vector_fields_2}}{=}

\displaystyle=\pounds_{\alpha\xi+\beta}\tau+\mathrm{d}(\alpha N)-\langle(\alpha\psi)\mathbin{\mathchoice{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\displaystyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\textstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}{\vtop{\halign{#\cr$\scriptscriptstyle\wedge$\cr\smash{\raisebox{-0.86108pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\cdot$}}\cr}}}}e\rangle\overset{\eqref{vector_fields_2}}{=}\dot{\tau}|_{\xi\mapsto\alpha\xi+\beta,\psi\mapsto\alpha\psi,N\mapsto\alpha N}\,.

Geometrically, this systematic reduction demonstrates that the full 4-dimensional spacetime symmetries project consistently down to the spatial hypersurface. The resulting field variations are equivalent to a combined spatial diffeomorphism along the modified shifted vector field
\alpha\xi+\beta
, supplemented by local internal gauge rotations parameterized by the fields
\alpha\Lambda
,
\alpha\psi
, and
\alpha N
.
